# Commands · M · part 2

1,420 entries, memory-history to model-3d. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **memory-init**: Initialize project memory by capturing non-obvious conventions, architectural patterns, and operational constraints that would waste time to rediscover. Triggers on: "initialise la mémoire", "analyse le projet et met en mémoire", "bootstrap project memory", "capture project context", "init project memory", "mémorise ce projet", "enregistre le contexte du projet".
